# Comprehensive Legal Classification of Drafts & Templates
This document provides a highly structured legal classification and navigable index of the **2165 templates** across the **54 subdirectories** in the drafts catalog. It organizes all documents into **9 Core Legal Domains** from a professional legal perspective to facilitate efficient discovery, categorization, and retrieval.

> [!NOTE]
> All listed files are **fully interactive** and renamed using Web-Safe lower kebab-case (`kebab-case`). In markdown-enabled editors (such as VS Code or Cursor), clicking any document name will directly open the corresponding template file in your workspace.

---

## Executive Summary & Statistical Breakdown
Below is a high-level summary of the drafts database partitioned by legal domain, along with the folders grouped under each and the respective document counts:

| Legal Domain | Included Subdirectories | Total Files | Domain Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1. Property & Real Estate Law** | `Sale`, `Rent`, `Conveyancing`, `Easements`, `Partition`, `Exchange`, `Gift`, `Release` | **159** | Governs the creation, transfer, lease, partition, and gifting of immovable property, land, and real estate assets. |
| **2. Corporate, Commercial & Contract Law** | `Company`, `Partnership`, `Franchisee`, `Memorandum of Understanding`, `Arbitration`, `Assignment`, `Indemnity`, `Guarantee`, `Agreement`, `Composition deed`, `Appranticeship`, `Hire-Purchase` | **416** | Covers corporate formation, business operations, commercial agreements, partnership deeds, franchising, and obligations like arbitration, guarantees, and indemnity. |
| **3. Civil Litigation, Procedure & Pleadings** | `Civil Pleadings`, `Pleadings`, `Plaint and Written statement`, `Petition`, `Notice`, `Affidavit`, `Application`, `Specific Relief Act`, `Vakalatnama`, `Acknowledgement`, `Consumer protection act` | **434** | Procedural and substantive templates for drafting plaints, written statements, affidavits, petitions, notices, and applications for civil courts, tribunals, and consumer forums. |
| **4. Criminal Law & Procedure** | `Criminal Pleading`, `Classification Of Offences`, `Motor Vehicle Act` | **143** | Covers criminal pleadings, bail applications, warnings, procedural actions, and claims under specialized penal acts like the Motor Vehicle Act. |
| **5. Family, Matrimonial & Personal Succession** | `Matrimonial`, `Adoption`, `Will`, `Trust, Wakf society` | **92** | Addresses personal status, family disputes, adoptions, succession planning, trusts, wills, and estate planning. |
| **6. Banking, Tax & Financial Securities** | `Banking`, `Bond`, `Income Tax`, `Lease Financing`, `Mortgage, Pledge , Hypothecation and Security Document`, `Negotiable Instrument`, `Infrastructure, development and financing` | **392** | Governs financing agreements, debt structures, tax compliance filings, mortgages, pledges, negotiable instruments, and financial bonds. |
| **7. Labor & Employment Law** | `Appointment`, `Labour` | **33** | Governs employer-employee relations, appointment letters, service agreements, and trade union/labor disputes. |
| **8. Intellectual Property & IT Law** | `Information Technology`, `Copyright` | **15** | Filings and contracts covering copyright protection, software licensing, website development, and technology transfers. |
| **9. General, Power of Attorney & Miscellaneous Deeds** | `Miscellaneous deeds`, `AGREEMENTS AND DRAFTS`, `Medical Form`, `Principle`, `Power of Attorney` | **481** | Catch-all category for power of attorney templates, general agreements, medical forms, and miscellaneous deeds of broad legal application. |
| **TOTALS** | **54 Subdirectories** | **2165 Files** | **Unified Drafts Database** |
